Method: 提出Slow-Fast Inference (SFI)框架：快速步骤重用紧凑的稀疏内存进行高效解码；慢速步骤在语义边界附近触发，重新审视更广泛的上下文并使用Selector刷新选定内存。

Result: 在评估的上下文长度范围内，SFI提供约1.6倍到14.4倍更高的解码吞吐量，同时在长上下文和长思维链设置中保持与完整KV基线相当的质量。

Conclusion: SFI无需训练且可直接应用于现有检查点，为当代自回归推理模型在长上下文、长视野和智能体工作负载中降低推理成本提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Long-context autoregressive decoding remains expensive because each decoding step must repeatedly process a growing history. We observe a consistent pattern during decoding: within a sentence, and more generally within a short semantically coherent span, the dominant attention support often remains largely stable. Motivated by this observation, we propose Slow-Fast Inference (SFI), a training-free decoding framework that decouples generation into frequent low-cost fast steps and occasional dense-attention slow steps. Fast steps reuse a compact sparse memory for efficient decoding. Slow steps are triggered near semantic boundaries. At slow steps, the model revisits the broader context and uses the Selector to refresh the selected memory for subsequent fast steps. Across the evaluated context lengths, SFI delivers approximately $1.6\times$--$14.4\times$ higher decoding throughput while generally maintaining quality on par with the full-KV baseline across long-context and long-CoT settings. Because SFI is training-free and applies directly to existing checkpoints, it offers a practical path to reducing inference cost for contemporary autoregressive reasoning models in long-context, long-horizon, and agentic workloads.

</details>


### [153] [Cross-Domain Policy Optimization via Bellman Consistency and Hybrid Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12087)
*Ming-Hong Chen,Kuan-Chen Pan,You-De Huang,Xi Liu,Ping-Chun Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QAvatar方法，通过跨域Bellman一致性和混合批评器解决跨域强化学习中的状态/动作空间差异和负迁移问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域强化学习面临两个核心挑战：1) 源域和目标域的状态/动作空间可能不同，需要复杂的域间映射；2) 源域模型的迁移性难以预先确定，容易产生负迁移效应

Method: 提出QAvatar方法，引入跨域Bellman一致性作为源域模型迁移性的度量，结合源域和目标域Q函数构建混合批评器，使用自适应无超参数权重函数

Result: 理论分析表明QAvatar具有收敛性并能实现可靠迁移，实验证明在运动控制和机器人手臂操作等基准任务中具有优越的迁移性能

Conclusion: QAvatar通过跨域Bellman一致性和混合批评器设计，有效解决了跨域强化学习中的域间差异和负迁移问题，实现了可靠的知识迁移

Abstract: Cross-domain reinforcement learning (CDRL) is meant to improve the data efficiency of RL by leveraging the data samples collected from a source domain to facilitate the learning in a similar target domain. Despite its potential, cross-domain transfer in RL is known to have two fundamental and intertwined challenges: (i) The source and target domains can have distinct state space or action space, and this makes direct transfer infeasible and thereby requires more sophisticated inter-domain mappings; (ii) The transferability of a source-domain model in RL is not easily identifiable a priori, and hence CDRL can be prone to negative effect during transfer. In this paper, we propose to jointly tackle these two challenges through the lens of \textit{cross-domain Bellman consistency} and \textit{hybrid critic}. Specifically, we first introduce the notion of cross-domain Bellman consistency as a way to measure transferability of a source-domain model. Then, we propose $Q$Avatar, which combines the Q functions from both the source and target domains with an adaptive hyperparameter-free weight function. Through this design, we characterize the convergence behavior of $Q$Avatar and show that $Q$Avatar achieves reliable transfer in the sense that it effectively leverages a source-domain Q function for knowledge transfer to the target domain. Through experiments, we demonstrate that $Q$Avatar achieves favorable transferability across various RL benchmark tasks, including locomotion and robot arm manipulation. Our code is available at https://rl-bandits-lab.github.io/Cross-Domain-RL/.

</details>


### [154] [Taming the Adversary: Stable Minimax Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient via Fractional Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12110)
*Taeho Lee,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MMDDPG框架，通过极小极大优化学习连续控制任务中的抗干扰策略，在用户策略和对抗性干扰策略之间建立平衡，显著提升RL智能体在外部扰动和模型不确定性下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在控制决策任务中取得显著成功，但智能体在面对意外外部干扰和模型不确定性时性能不稳定或下降。确保在这些条件下的可靠性能仍然是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出极小极大深度确定性策略梯度（MMDDPG）框架，将训练过程建模为用户策略和对抗性干扰策略之间的极小极大优化问题。用户学习最小化目标函数的鲁棒策略，而对抗方生成最大化目标函数的干扰。引入分数目标函数平衡任务性能和干扰幅度，防止过度激进的干扰并促进鲁棒学习。

Result: 在MuJoCo环境中的实验评估表明，提出的MMDDPG在对抗外部力扰动和模型参数变化方面实现了显著改善的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MMDDPG框架通过极小极大优化有效学习抗干扰策略，解决了RL智能体在不确定环境中的鲁棒性问题，为连续控制任务提供了可靠的抗干扰解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved remarkable success in a wide range of control and decision-making tasks. However, RL agents often exhibit unstable or degraded performance when deployed in environments subject to unexpected external disturbances and model uncertainties. Consequently, ensuring reliable performance under such conditions remains a critical challenge. In this paper, we propose minimax deep deterministic policy gradient (MMDDPG), a framework for learning disturbance-resilient policies in continuous control tasks. The training process is formulated as a minimax optimization problem between a user policy and an adversarial disturbance policy. In this problem, the user learns a robust policy that minimizes the objective function, while the adversary generates disturbances that maximize it. To stabilize this interaction, we introduce a fractional objective that balances task performance and disturbance magnitude. This objective prevents excessively aggressive disturbances and promotes robust learning. Experimental evaluations in MuJoCo environments demonstrate that the proposed MMDDPG achieves significantly improved robustness against both external force perturbations and model parameter variations.

</details>


### [155] [Cornserve: A Distributed Serving System for Any-to-Any Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12118)
*Jae-Won Chung,Jeff J. Ma,Jisang Ahn,Yizhuo Liang,Akshay Jajoo,Myungjin Lee,Mosharaf Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cornserve是一个用于通用Any-to-Any模型的分布式服务系统，通过组件解耦和独立扩展实现高效服务，相比现有方案提升3.81倍吞吐量并降低5.79倍尾部延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Any-to-Any模型作为新兴的多模态模型，能够接受和生成多种模态数据组合（文本、图像、视频、音频）。这类模型的服务面临挑战：不同请求的输入输出模态不同，导致计算图路径不同；模型各组件具有不同的扩展特性。现有服务系统难以有效处理这种异构性和动态性。

Method: Cornserve采用灵活的task抽象来表达Any-to-Any模型计算图，支持组件解耦和独立扩展。分布式运行时通过高效的记录-重放执行模型调度计算到数据平面，跟踪数据依赖关系，并直接在生产者与消费者之间转发张量数据。系统基于Kubernetes构建，使用约23K行Python代码实现。

Result: Cornserve支持多样化的Any-to-Any模型，相比现有方案能够提供高达3.81倍的吞吐量提升和5.79倍的尾部延迟降低。系统已开源，演示视频可在YouTube上获取。

Conclusion: Cornserve通过创新的任务抽象和分布式执行模型，有效解决了Any-to-Any模型服务的挑战，实现了组件解耦、独立扩展和高效数据转发，为多模态AI模型的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Any-to-Any models are an emerging class of multimodal models that accept combinations of multimodal data (e.g., text, image, video, audio) as input and generate them as output. Serving these models are challenging; different requests with different input and output modalities traverse different paths through the model computation graph, and each component of the model have different scaling characteristics.
  We present Cornserve, a distributed serving system for generic Any-to-Any models. Cornserve provides a flexible task abstraction for expressing Any-to-Any model computation graphs, enabling component disaggregation and independent scaling. The distributed runtime dispatches compute to the data plane via an efficient record-and-replay execution model that keeps track of data dependencies, and forwards tensor data between components directly from the producer to the consumer. Built on Kubernetes with approximately 23K new lines of Python, Cornserve supports diverse Any-to-Any models and delivers up to 3.81$\times$ higher throughput and 5.79$\times$ lower tail latency. Cornserve is open-source, and the demo video is available on YouTube.

</details>


### [156] [IsoCompute Playbook: Optimally Scaling Sampling Compute for LLM RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12151)
*Zhoujun Cheng,Yutao Xie,Yuxiao Qu,Amrith Setlur,Shibo Hao,Varad Pimpalkhute,Tongtong Liang,Feng Yao,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Virginia Smith,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,Zhiting Hu,Taylor Killian,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了LLM强化学习后训练的计算最优分配，发现并行rollout数量随计算预算增加而可预测地增长并最终饱和，这一趋势在简单和困难问题上由不同机制驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然扩展定律指导了LLM预训练的计算分配，但针对LLM强化学习后训练的类似指导原则仍然缺乏理解。研究者希望建立RL后训练的计算最优分配规则，为高效计算利用提供指导。

Method: 将RL扩展建模为计算约束下的优化问题，考虑三个资源维度：每个问题的并行rollout数量、每批次的问题数量、更新步数。研究计算最优的并行rollout数量如何随计算预算变化，并分析简单和困难问题上的不同机制。

Result: 发现计算最优的并行rollout数量随计算预算增加而可预测地增长并最终饱和。在简单问题上，这一趋势由解决方案锐化驱动；在困难问题上，由覆盖扩展驱动。增加并行rollout数量能减轻问题间的干扰，而每批次问题数量主要影响训练稳定性且可在较宽范围内选择。

Conclusion: 研究结果将RL扩展定律重新定义为规定性分配规则，为计算高效的LLM RL后训练提供了实用指导。这些发现在不同基础模型和数据分布上得到验证，有助于优化RL后训练的计算资源分配。

Abstract: While scaling laws guide compute allocation for LLM pre-training, analogous prescriptions for reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) remain poorly understood. We study the compute-optimal allocation of sampling compute for on-policy RL methods in LLMs, framing scaling as a compute-constrained optimization over three resources: parallel rollouts per problem, number of problems per batch, and number of update steps. We find that the compute-optimal number of parallel rollouts per problem increases predictably with compute budget and then saturates. This trend holds across both easy and hard problems, though driven by different mechanisms: solution sharpening on easy problems and coverage expansion on hard problems. We further show that increasing the number of parallel rollouts mitigates interference across problems, while the number of problems per batch primarily affects training stability and can be chosen within a broad range. Validated across base models and data distributions, our results recast RL scaling laws as prescriptive allocation rules and provide practical guidance for compute-efficient LLM RL post-training.

</details>


### [157] [A Quantitative Characterization of Forgetting in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12163)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从理论角度分析了生成模型持续后训练中的遗忘现象，提出了质量遗忘和旧分量漂移两种遗忘形式，并证明不同KL散度目标在遗忘行为上存在本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型的持续后训练被广泛使用，但对于何时以及为何发生遗忘现象缺乏原理性理解。现有研究对遗忘机制的理论分析不足，需要建立量化框架来理解遗忘的成因和条件。

Method: 采用两模态混合抽象（代表旧任务和新任务）的理论框架，形式化定义质量遗忘和旧分量漂移两种遗忘形式。在等协方差高斯模态假设下，分析前向KL和后向KL目标函数的遗忘行为，量化重放机制与目标函数的交互作用，并分析三种近期提出的近策略后训练方法。

Result: 前向KL目标训练新分布数据会导致旧权重归零（质量遗忘），而后向KL目标收敛到真实目标（避免质量遗忘），旧均值漂移受Bhattacharyya系数控制的重叠门控错配概率影响，漂移随模态分离指数衰减。重放机制对前向KL需要修改训练分布，对后向KL则通过有界重要性加权防止旧模态饥饿。三种近策略方法在特定条件下能保留旧质量并表现出重叠控制漂移。

Conclusion: 遗忘现象可以基于散度方向、几何行为重叠、采样机制以及训练过程中过去行为的可见性之间的相互作用进行精确量化。后向KL目标在避免质量遗忘方面具有优势，而漂移现象受模态重叠程度控制。

Abstract: Continual post-training of generative models is widely used, yet a principled understanding of when and why forgetting occurs remains limited. We develop theoretical results under a two-mode mixture abstraction (representing old and new tasks), proposed by Chen et al. (2025) (arXiv:2510.18874), and formalize forgetting in two forms: (i) mass forgetting, where the old mixture weight collapses to zero, and (ii) old-component drift, where an already-correct old component shifts during training. For equal-covariance Gaussian modes, we prove that forward-KL objectives trained on data from the new distribution drive the old weight to zero, while reverse-KL objectives converge to the true target (thereby avoiding mass forgetting) and perturb the old mean only through overlap-gated misassignment probabilities controlled by the Bhattacharyya coefficient, yielding drift that decays exponentially with mode separation and a locally well-conditioned geometry with exponential convergence. We further quantify how replay interacts with these objectives. For forward-KL, replay must modify the training distribution to change the population optimum; for reverse-KL, replay leaves the population objective unchanged but prevents finite-batch old-mode starvation through bounded importance weighting. Finally, we analyze three recently proposed near-on-policy post-training methods, SDFT (arxiv:2601.19897), TTT-Discover (arxiv:2601.16175), and OAPL (arxiv:2602.19362), via the same lens and derive explicit conditions under which each retains old mass and exhibits overlap-controlled drift. Overall, our results show that forgetting can by precisely quantified based on the interaction between divergence direction, geometric behavioral overlap, sampling regime, and the visibility of past behavior during training.

</details>


### [158] [Neural Thickets: Diverse Task Experts Are Dense Around Pretrained Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12228)
*Yulu Gan,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将预训练结果视为参数向量分布而非单一向量，发现大模型中任务专家密度显著增加，从而提出基于随机采样和投票的并行后训练方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上预训练被看作产生单一参数向量作为后续优化的起点，但作者认为预训练实际上产生了一个参数向量分布，其中已经包含了任务特定的专家解。这种视角转变有助于理解为什么大模型更容易通过简单方法找到好的任务解决方案。

Method: 提出一种完全并行的后训练方法：1) 从预训练权重周围随机采样N个参数扰动；2) 选择表现最好的K个；3) 通过多数投票集成这些专家的预测。该方法避免了复杂的优化过程如梯度下降。

Result: 该方法在当代大规模模型上表现优异，与标准后训练方法如PPO、GRPO和ES相比具有竞争力。实验表明大模型中任务专家密度显著高于小模型，使得随机采样方法能够有效发现高质量专家。

Conclusion: 预训练应被视为产生参数向量分布而非单一向量，大模型中任务专家密度显著增加使得简单随机采样方法能够有效发现高质量解决方案。这种新视角为后训练提供了更简单高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Pretraining produces a learned parameter vector that is typically treated as a starting point for further iterative adaptation. In this work, we instead view the outcome of pretraining as a distribution over parameter vectors, whose support already contains task-specific experts. We show that in small models such expert solutions occupy a negligible fraction of the volume of this distribution, making their discovery reliant on structured optimization methods such as gradient descent. In contrast, in large, well-pretrained models the density of task-experts increases dramatically, so that diverse, task-improving specialists populate a substantial fraction of the neighborhood around the pretrained weights. Motivated by this perspective, we explore a simple, fully parallel post-training method that samples $N$ parameter perturbations at random, selects the top $K$, and ensembles predictions via majority vote. Despite its simplicity, this approach is competitive with standard post-training methods such as PPO, GRPO, and ES for contemporary large-scale models.

</details>


### [159] [STAMP: Selective Task-Aware Mechanism for Text Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12237)
*Fengwei Tian,Payel Bhattacharjee,Heidi Hanson,Geoffrey D. Rubin,Joseph Y. Lo,Ravi Tandon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAMP框架通过选择性分配隐私预算和极坐标机制，在文本隐私保护中实现了更好的隐私-效用权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本隐私保护方法通常在隐私保护和下游任务效用之间存在权衡问题，需要一种能够根据任务相关性和隐私敏感性进行细粒度控制的机制

Method: STAMP框架通过两个关键组件：1）基于token任务重要性和隐私敏感性的选择性隐私预算分配；2）极坐标机制，仅扰动嵌入向量的方向而保持幅度不变，通过余弦最近邻搜索进行解码

Result: 在SQuAD、Yelp和AG News数据集上的实验表明，STAMP结合归一化极坐标机制在不同token级隐私预算下均实现了优越的隐私-效用权衡

Conclusion: STAMP通过细粒度的token级隐私预算分配和几何对齐的扰动机制，为任务感知的文本隐私保护提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: We present STAMP (Selective Task-Aware Mechanism for Text Privacy), a new framework for task-aware text privatization that achieves an improved privacy-utility trade-off. STAMP selectively allocates privacy budgets across tokens by jointly considering (i) each token's importance to the downstream task (as measured via a task- or query-specific representation), and (ii) its privacy sensitivity (e.g., names, dates, identifiers). This token-level partitioning enables fine-grained, group-wise control over the level of noise applied to different parts of the input, balancing privacy protection with task relevance. To privatize individual token embeddings, we introduce the polar mechanism, which perturbs only the direction of embeddings on the unit sphere while preserving their magnitude. Decoding is performed via cosine nearest-neighbor search, aligning the perturbation geometry with the decoding geometry. Unlike isotropic noise mechanisms, the polar mechanism maintains semantic neighborhoods in the embedding space and better preserves downstream utility. Experimental evaluations on SQuAD, Yelp, and AG News datasets demonstrate that STAMP, when combined with the normalized polar mechanism, consistently achieves superior privacy-utility trade-offs across varying per-token privacy budgets.

</details>


### [160] [Matching Features, Not Tokens: Energy-Based Fine-Tuning of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12248)
*Samy Jelassi,Mujin Kwun,Rosie Zhao,Yuanzhi Li,Nicolo Fusi,Yilun Du,Sham M. Kakade,Carles Domingo-Enrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EBFT是一种基于特征匹配的语言模型微调方法，通过并行采样生成多个rollout，提取序列级特征进行策略梯度更新，在保持低交叉熵的同时提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉熵训练虽然提供密集监督，但只优化教师强制下的下一个token预测，而非模型rollout下的序列级行为。需要一种能提供密集语义反馈但不需要任务特定验证器或偏好模型的方法。

Method: 提出基于能量的微调（EBFT）：1）使用跨步块并行采样从嵌套前缀并发生成多个rollout；2）在这些rollout上批量提取特征；3）使用得到的嵌入执行在线策略梯度更新。该方法连接了KL正则化特征匹配和基于能量的建模。

Result: 在问答编码、非结构化编码和翻译任务上，EBFT匹配RLVR并优于SFT的下游准确率，同时验证交叉熵低于两种方法。

Conclusion: EBFT通过特征匹配目标提供序列级监督，实现了在保持低交叉熵的同时提升下游任务性能的有效微调方法。

Abstract: Cross-entropy (CE) training provides dense and scalable supervision for language models, but it optimizes next-token prediction under teacher forcing rather than sequence-level behavior under model rollouts. We introduce a feature-matching objective for language-model fine-tuning that targets sequence-level statistics of the completion distribution, providing dense semantic feedback without requiring a task-specific verifier or preference model. To optimize this objective efficiently, we propose energy-based fine-tuning (EBFT), which uses strided block-parallel sampling to generate multiple rollouts from nested prefixes concurrently, batches feature extraction over these rollouts, and uses the resulting embeddings to perform an on-policy policy-gradient update. We present a theoretical perspective connecting EBFT to KL-regularized feature-matching and energy-based modeling. Empirically, across Q&A coding, unstructured coding, and translation, EBFT matches RLVR and outperforms SFT on downstream accuracy while achieving a lower validation cross-entropy than both methods.

</details>


### [161] [The Latent Color Subspace: Emergent Order in High-Dimensional Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12261)
*Mateusz Pach,Jessica Bader,Quentin Bouniot,Serge Belongie,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种解释FLUX.1变分自编码器潜在空间中颜色表示的方法，揭示了反映色调、饱和度和亮度的结构，并基于此开发了无需训练的显式颜色控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管文本到图像生成模型发展迅速，但实现细粒度控制仍然困难，主要原因是缺乏对语义信息编码方式的理解。本文旨在解释FLUX.1变分自编码器潜在空间中的颜色表示机制。

Method: 开发了潜在颜色子空间（LCS）解释方法，揭示了潜在空间中反映色调、饱和度和亮度的结构。基于此提出了一种完全无需训练的显式颜色控制方法，仅通过闭式潜在空间操作实现。

Result: 验证了LCS解释的有效性，证明其既能预测又能显式控制生成图像的颜色。该方法在FLUX模型上实现了基于潜在空间操作的训练无关颜色控制。

Conclusion: 成功解释了FLUX.1潜在空间中的颜色表示结构，并开发了无需训练的显式颜色控制方法，为理解生成模型的语义编码提供了新视角，代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation models have advanced rapidly, yet achieving fine-grained control over generated images remains difficult, largely due to limited understanding of how semantic information is encoded. We develop an interpretation of the color representation in the Variational Autoencoder latent space of FLUX.1 [Dev], revealing a structure reflecting Hue, Saturation, and Lightness. We verify our Latent Color Subspace (LCS) interpretation by demonstrating that it can both predict and explicitly control color, introducing a fully training-free method in FLUX based solely on closed-form latent-space manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/LCS.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [From Computational Certification to Exact Coordinates: Heilbronn's Triangle Problem on the Unit Square Using Mixed-Integer Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11107)
*Nathan Sudermann-Merx*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出优化-精化框架解决Heilbronn三角形问题，结合混合整数非线性规划和精确符号计算，显著提升计算效率，首次证明n=9配置的全局最优性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典Heilbronn三角形问题的计算挑战，特别是n=9情况的证明需要大量计算时间（约一天），需要更高效的优化方法和严格的数学证明

Method: 开发优化-精化框架，结合全局混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）和精确符号计算；采用新颖的对称性破缺策略，利用行列式的结构特性建立更强的优化模型

Result: n=9问题可在15分钟内求解到全局最优，比之前的一天计算时间提升一个数量级；首次证明Comellas和Yebra在2002年发现的n=9配置是全局最优的；为n=5-9提供精确坐标配置

Conclusion: 提出的框架显著提高了Heilbronn三角形问题的求解效率，首次严格证明了n=9的全局最优性；发现的配置结构模式（非关键三角形面积聚集在少数值周围）为极值点集的组合几何研究提出了新问题

Abstract: We develop an optimize-then-refine framework for the classical Heilbronn triangle problem that integrates global mixed-integer nonlinear programming with exact symbolic computation. A novel symmetry-breaking strategy, together with the exploitation of structural properties of determinants, yields a substantially stronger optimization model: for $n=9$, the problem can be solved to certified global optimality in 15 minutes on a standard desktop computer, improving upon the previously reported effort of about one day by more than an order of magnitude. Combining the numerical certificate with exact symbolic computation, we provide the first proof that the configuration discovered by Comellas and Yebra in 2002 for $n=9$ is globally optimal, and derive exact coordinates for all optimal configurations with $n=5,\dots,9$, confirming earlier best-known results and sometimes simplifying their presentation. An analysis of these configurations reveals structural patterns-notably the clustering of noncritical triangle areas around a small number of distinct values-which give rise to new research questions about the combinatorial geometry of extremal point sets. All configurations and code are publicly available to provide a reproducible foundation for further research.

</details>


### [163] [First and second-order optimality conditions for a bilinear controlled wave equation on an infinite horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11271)
*Redouane El Mezegueldy,Zakarya Dardour*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无限时间域上双线性阻尼波动方程的最优控制问题，建立了控制系统的适定性，证明了最优控制的存在性，并推导了一阶和二阶最优性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限时间域上双线性阻尼波动方程的最优控制问题，这类系统在物理和工程应用中具有重要意义，但无限时间域上的最优控制分析具有挑战性，需要建立完整的数学理论框架。

Method: 首先建立控制系统的适定性和一致性能量估计；通过构造极小化序列证明最优控制的存在性；证明控制到状态映射的二阶Fréchet可微性；推导一阶必要条件（变分不等式和点态投影公式）；建立二阶必要和充分条件（Hessian矩阵的非负性和强制性）。

Result: 证明了无限时间域上双线性阻尼波动方程最优控制的存在性；推导了一阶最优性条件（变分不等式和点态投影公式）；建立了二阶最优性条件（Hessian非负性为必要条件，强制性为充分条件）；完整刻画了有界空间域上无限时间域双线性双曲控制系统局部最优性的特征。

Conclusion: 该论文为无限时间域上双线性阻尼波动方程的最优控制问题提供了完整的数学理论框架，包括适定性、最优控制存在性、一阶和二阶最优性条件，为这类系统的分析和应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper investigates the optimal control of a bilinear damped wave equation over an infinite time horizon. We establish the well-posedness of the controlled system and derive uniform energy estimates. The existence of optimal controls is proven by constructing a minimizing sequence. We prove that the control-to-state mapping is twice continuously Fréchet differentiable, which enables the derivation of first-order necessary optimality conditions in the form of a variational inequality and a pointwise projection formula. Furthermore, we establish second-order necessary and sufficient conditions: the nonnegativity of the Hessian of the cost functional is shown to be a necessary condition for local optimality, while the coercivity of this Hessian constitutes a sufficient condition. These results provide a complete characterization of local optimality for bilinear hyperbolic control systems over infinite time horizons on bounded spatial domains.

</details>


### [164] [Minimizers that are not Impulsive Minimizers and Higher Order Abnormality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11302)
*Monica Motta,Michele Palladino,Franco Rampazzo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究最优控制中的两个相关问题：1) 解决集合分离方法与惩罚技术之间的兼容性问题，通过QDQ逼近锥与Clarke切锥的关系；2) 将兼容性结果应用于无界控制和脉冲扩展问题中的下确界间隙现象，建立严格意义极小化器的异常性与高阶极大值原理的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在解决最优控制理论中的两个核心问题：首先，针对具有最终目标的最优控制问题，集合分离方法与惩罚技术这两种经典方法通常导出不等价的条件，主要源于它们对目标处切性概念的不同定义。其次，研究无界控制和脉冲扩展问题中的下确界间隙现象，特别是严格意义极小化器的异常性与高阶极大值原理之间的关系。

Method: 第一部分：引入拟微分商(QDQ)逼近锥（适用于集合分离方法），并确定Clarke切锥（惩罚技术的典型工具）也是QDQ逼近锥的条件。特别地，在适当的局部不变性假设下，或当目标局部与r-邻近正则集重合时，证明该性质成立。第二部分：将兼容性结果应用于无界控制和脉冲扩展问题，通过拓扑论证和前述兼容性问题的利用，建立严格意义极小化器的下确界间隙与涉及李括号的高阶极大值原理中异常性的对应关系。

Result: 1) 证明了在局部不变性假设或目标局部与r-邻近正则集重合的条件下，Clarke切锥也是QDQ逼近锥，从而解决了两种方法的兼容性问题。2) 建立了严格意义极小化器的下确界间隙与高阶极大值原理中异常性的对应关系，这一结果此前仅对扩展意义（脉冲）极小化器已知，现通过拓扑论证和兼容性分析扩展到严格意义极小化器。

Conclusion: 该论文成功解决了最优控制中两种经典方法之间的兼容性问题，并将这一理论进展应用于下确界间隙现象的研究。通过建立Clarke切锥与QDQ逼近锥的关系，以及严格意义极小化器的异常性与高阶极大值原理的联系，为最优控制理论提供了重要的理论工具和洞察，特别是在处理无界控制和脉冲扩展问题时。

Abstract: This paper addresses two related problems in optimal control. The first investigation consists of compatibility issues between two classical approaches to deriving necessary conditions for optimal control problems with a final target: the set-separation approach and penalization techniques. These methods generally lead to non-equivalent conditions, mainly due to their reliance on different notions of tangency at the target.
  We address this issue by considering Quasi Differential Quotient (QDQ) approximating cones (which are fit for the set-separation approach) and identifying conditions under which the Clarke tangent cone (which is a typical tool within penalization techniques) is also a QDQ approximating cone. In particular, we show that this property holds under suitable local invariance assumptions or when the target coincides locally with an $r$-prox regular set.
  In the second part of the paper we apply this compatibility result to the study of infimum-gap phenomena in optimal control problems with unbounded controls and impulsive extensions. In particular, we establish a connection between the occurrence of infimum gaps for strict-sense minimizers and abnormality in a higher-order Maximum Principle involving Lie brackets. While the abnormality-gap correspondence beyond first-order conditions has been already established for extended-sense --i.e. impulsive-- minimizers, a topological argument involving the former and the utilization of the above compatibility issues allow us to extend this correspondence to strict-sense minimizers.

</details>


### [165] [Blind Hyperspectral and Multispectral Images Fusion: A Unified Tensor Fusion Framework from Coupled Inverse Problem Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11530)
*Ying Gao,Michael K. Ng,Chunfeng cui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种盲融合框架，将高光谱与多光谱图像融合问题建模为空间域盲去卷积和光谱域盲解混的耦合逆问题，无需已知空间模糊算子和光谱响应算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高光谱与多光谱图像融合方法通常假设空间模糊算子和光谱响应算子已知，但这一假设在实际应用中极为受限。为解决这一限制，需要开发能够在算子未知情况下进行融合的方法。

Method: 将盲融合问题建模为耦合逆问题，提出统一的张量融合框架，构建联合估计目标高分辨率高光谱图像、空间点扩散函数和光谱响应函数的优化模型。采用带有Moreau包络平滑的部分线性化交替方向乘子法求解，并设计了针对融合问题特性的初始化估计器。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上与最先进方法进行数值比较，证明了所提方法的优越性能。该方法能够灵活自调整、实时融合且无需预训练。

Conclusion: 通过将盲融合问题重新表述为空间域盲去卷积和光谱域盲解混的耦合逆问题，提出了一种有效的统一张量融合框架，能够在算子未知的情况下实现高性能的高光谱与多光谱图像融合。

Abstract: Hyperspectral and multispectral images fusion aims at integrating a low-resolution hyperspectral image (LR-HSI) and a high-resolution multispectral image (HR-MSI) to construct a high-resolution hyperspectral image (HR-HSI). It is generally assumed that spatial blurring operator and spectral response operator are prior-known. However, such an assumption is extremely restrictive in practice. To overcome this limitation, this paper formulates blind fusion as a coupled inverse problem, integrating blind deconvolution in the spatial domain with blind unmixing in the spectral domain. From this novel perspective, we propose a unified tensor fusion framework capable of flexible self-adjustment and real-time fusion without pre-training. We further introduce an optimization model for the joint estimation of the target HR-HSI, the spatial point spread function, and the spectral response function. To solve this model, we devise a partially linearized alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) algorithm with Moreau envelope smoothing, accompanied by the rigorous convergence analysis. An initialization estimator tailored to the specific characteristics of the fusion problem is proposed. Numerical comparisons with state-of-the-art methods on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate the compelling performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [166] [Simultaneous estimation of multiple discrete unimodal distributions under stochastic order constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11532)
*Yasuhiro Yoshida,Noriyoshi Sukegawa,Jiro Iwanaga*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种估计多个离散单峰分布的方法，通过引入随机序约束来利用分布间的先验关系，将问题建模为混合整数凸二次优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受现实世界平台搜索行为分析的驱动，需要估计多个离散单峰分布。为了利用分布间优先关系的先验知识，引入随机序约束来改进估计效果。

Method: 将估计任务建模为混合整数凸二次优化问题，通过施加随机序约束来整合分布间的先验关系，实现对多个离散单峰分布的联合估计。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，当样本量较小时，该方法平均减少Jensen-Shannon散度2.2%（最高达6.3%）；当数据充足时，与现有方法表现相当。

Conclusion: 提出的随机序约束方法在小样本情况下能有效提升多个离散单峰分布的估计精度，同时在大样本情况下保持与现有方法相当的性能。

Abstract: We study the problem of estimating multiple discrete unimodal distributions, motivated by search behavior analysis on a real-world platform. To incorporate prior knowledge of precedence relations among distributions, we impose stochastic order constraints and formulate the estimation task as a mixed-integer convex quadratic optimization problem. Experiments on both synthetic and real datasets show that the proposed method reduces the Jensen-Shannon divergence by 2.2% on average (up to 6.3%) when the sample size is small, while performing comparably to existing methods when sufficient data are available.

</details>


### [167] [Families of Two-Impulse Optimal Rendezvous Transfers Between Elliptic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11538)
*Beom Park,Kathleen C. Howell,Jaewoo Kim,Jaemyung Ahn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 重新审视经典燃料最优双脉冲交会问题，从族系角度揭示最优解之间的连续连接关系，而非孤立解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法得到的燃料最优双脉冲交会解往往是孤立的，最优解之间的相互关系不明确。本文旨在揭示这些看似无关的最优解实际上是连续解族的成员，从而提供对解空间全局结构的理解。

Method: 通过重新参数化问题，强制执行一阶必要最优条件子集，利用数值延拓追踪单参数解族。使用基于Hessian矩阵的准则和Primer Vector理论对解族进行分类，并将解族投影到porkchop图上连接角度域和时间域。

Result: 代表性案例研究揭示了局部最优分支在轨道几何变化下的出现、合并和消失现象，提供了解空间的全局映射。该方法阐明了最优解的鲁棒性，并能在标称轨迹附近识别替代的近最优转移方案。

Conclusion: 从族系角度分析燃料最优双脉冲交会问题提供了对解空间结构的补充视角，揭示了最优解之间的连续连接关系，有助于理解解的鲁棒性和寻找替代的近似最优转移方案。

Abstract: The classical fuel-optimal two-impulse rendezvous problem between Keplerian orbits is revisited from a family-based perspective. Conventional approaches often yield isolated optimal solutions whose mutual relationships remain unclear; yet, when re-parameterized appropriately, seemingly unrelated optima are revealed to be connected members of continuous solution families. To expose this structure, the proposed framework enforces a subset of first-order necessary optimality conditions and traces the resulting one-parameter families via numerical continuation. The families are classified using Hessian-based criteria and Primer Vector Theory, and are projected onto porkchop plots to connect the angular and temporal domains. Representative case studies reveal the emergence, merging, and disappearance of locally optimal branches under variations in orbital geometry, supplying a global map of the solution landscape. This complementary perspective clarifies the robustness of optimal solutions and identifies alternative near-optimal transfers in the vicinity of a nominal trajectory.

</details>


### [168] [Exploiting Parallelism in a QPALM-based Solver for Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11723)
*Pieter Pas,Kristoffer Fink Løwenstein,Daniele Bernardini,Panagiotis Patrinos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: QPALM-OCP算法针对最优控制中的二次规划问题，通过利用问题阶段独立性实现并行化和向量化优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优控制问题中的二次规划计算量大，QPALM-OCP算法具有阶段独立性特点，为并行化提供了机会以提升计算效率

Method: 利用最优控制问题中不同阶段可独立计算的结构特点，应用并行化和向量化技术，在C++中实现优化版本

Result: 提供了最优控制基准问题的结果，并与原始QPALM方法进行了比较，展示了性能提升

Conclusion: 通过利用问题特定结构实现并行化和向量化，QPALM-OCP算法能有效提升最优控制中二次规划问题的求解效率

Abstract: We discuss the opportunities for parallelization in the recently proposed QPALM-OCP algorithm, a solver tailored to quadratic programs arising in optimal control. A significant part of the computational work can be carried out independently for the different stages in the optimal control problem. We exploit this specific structure to apply parallelization and vectorization techniques in an optimized C++ implementation of the method. Results for optimal control benchmark problems and comparisons to the original QPALM method are provided.

</details>


### [169] [An Eikonal Approach for Globally Optimal Free Flight Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11830)
*Ralf Borndörfer,Arturas Jocas,Martin Weiser*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于程函方程的方法，用于在静止风场中寻找飞机的连续全局最优轨迹，以最小化排放和燃料消耗。通过构建割迹周围的信任区域来保证远离割迹的目的地轨迹唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目的地接近Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程的割迹时，小的数值离散化误差可能导致选择局部最优而非全局最优轨迹。需要解决数值方法在割迹附近的不稳定性问题，确保找到真正的全局最优轨迹。

Method: 采用基于程函方程的方法，结合有限元误差估计，在割迹周围构建信任区域。该方法保证对于足够远离割迹的目的地，轨迹具有唯一性，从而避免因数值误差而错过全局最优解。

Result: 该方法能够在静止风场中找到飞机的连续全局最优轨迹，有效最小化排放和燃料消耗。通过信任区域机制，解决了割迹附近数值不稳定性问题，确保全局最优性。

Conclusion: 提出的eikonal-based方法结合信任区域策略，能够可靠地找到全局最优轨迹，克服了传统数值方法在割迹附近可能选择局部最优解的问题，为飞机轨迹优化提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: We present an eikonal-based approach that is capable of finding a continuous globally optimal trajectory for an aircraft in a stationary wind field. This minimizes emissions and fuel consumption. If the destination is close to a cut locus of the associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation, small numerical discretization errors can lead to selecting a merely locally optimal trajectory and missing the globally optimal one. Based on finite element error estimates, we construct a trust region around the cut loci in order to guarantee uniqueness of trajectories for destinations sufficiently far from cut loci.

</details>


### [170] [Generalisation of Farkas' lemma beyond closedness: a constructive approach via Fenchel-Rockafellar duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11859)
*Camille Pouchol,Emmanuel Trélat,Christophe Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于优化对偶和Fenchel-Rockafellar对偶理论的新方法，推广Farkas引理，仅需锥P由闭有界凸集生成，无需A(P)闭性假设


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Farkas引理要求A(P)闭，但这一条件难以验证且不总是成立。现有推广大多仍要求A(P)闭或P闭，需要更弱的假设条件来扩展Farkas引理的应用范围

Method: 基于原始-对偶优化问题对和Fenchel-Rockafellar对偶理论，仅假设P由闭有界凸集生成。通过最优性条件构造性地刻画满足‖Ax-b‖≤ε的x∈P，并讨论对偶问题解的存在条件

Result: 获得了b∈A(P)和b∈A(P)闭包的必要充分条件。对于ε≥0，构造性地描述了满足近似解的x∈P。当ε=0时，解的存在性与对偶问题解的存在性密切相关

Conclusion: 新方法在更弱的假设下推广了Farkas引理，允许P和A(P)非闭。通过松弛技术还可应用于非凸锥，扩展了Farkas引理的应用范围

Abstract: Farkas' lemma is an ubiquitous tool in optimisation, as it provides necessary and sufficient conditions to have $b \in A(P)$, where $P$ is a closed convex cone, $A$ is a (continuous) linear mapping and $b$ is a fixed vector. The standard underlying hypothesis is the closedness of $A(P)$, which is not always satisfied and can be difficult to check. We devise a new method to generalise Farkas' lemma, based on a primal-dual pair of optimisation problems and Fenchel-Rockafellar duality theory. We work under the sole hypothesis that $P$ be generated by a closed bounded convex set. This hypothesis is weaker than in previous generalisations of Farkas' lemma, which almost all require that $A(P)$ be closed, or, in few cases, that only $P$ be closed. In our case, $P$ (and a fortiori $A(P)$) is not necessarily closed; we uncover necessary and sufficient conditions both for $b \in A(P)$ and $b \in \overline{A(P)}$. For a given $\e \geq 0$, we exhibit constructive characterisations of $x \in P$ such that $\|Ax-b\| \leq \e$ when it exists, by means of optimality conditions. For $\e = 0$, these strongly rely on whether the dual problem admits a solution, and we discuss conditions under which it does. Finally, we also explain how, upon relaxation, we may apply our method to a nonconvex cone.

</details>


### [171] [Asymptotically Efficient Recursive Identification Under One-Bit Communications Achieving Original CRLB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11964)
*Xingrui Liu,Jieming Ke,Mingjie Shao,Yanlong Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于一比特通信下自回归外生系统的渐近高效递归辨识算法，该算法能渐近达到基于原始量化前数据的CRLB，相比现有方法减少至少36%的渐近均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的一比特通信系统辨识方法通常只量化当前系统输出，导致在量化过程中存在不可忽略的信息损失，只能达到基于量化观测的CRLB，无法达到基于原始数据的CRLB。需要开发一种能减少量化信息损失、达到原始CRLB的高效辨识算法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的量化方法，将当前和历史系统输出与输入相结合进行量化，在一比特数据中提供更丰富的参数信息。基于此技术开发相应的远程估计算法，并建立新的分析框架来处理一比特数据的非独立性，通过分析量化前组合数据的尾概率来替代传统的独立性假设。

Result: 理论证明远程估计达到渐近正态性，误差协方差矩阵收敛到原始CRLB，确认了算法的渐近效率。相比现有的一比特数据辨识算法，该方法减少至少36%的渐近均方误差。数值模拟验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过结合当前和历史数据进行量化，成功开发出一种能渐近达到原始CRLB的高效一比特通信系统辨识算法，显著减少了量化引起的信息损失，为受限通信环境下的系统辨识提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper develops an asymptotically efficient recursive identification algorithm for autoregressive systems with exogenous inputs under one-bit communications. In particular, the proposed method asymptotically achieves the Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) based on the original data before quantization (original CRLB), whereas existing approaches typically attain only the CRLB corresponding to the quantized observations. The primary reason is that the existing methods quantize only the current system output, resulting in non-negligible information loss under one-bit quantization. To overcome this challenge, we present a novel quantization method that integrates both current and historical system outputs and inputs to provide richer parameter information in one-bit data, allowing the information loss caused by quantization to become a minor term relative to the original CRLB. Based on this technique, a corresponding remote estimation algorithm is further proposed. To address the convergence analysis challenge posed by the non-independence of the one-bit data, we establish a new framework that analyzes the tail probability of integrated data formed by combining current and historical system outputs and inputs before quantization, thereby eliminating the need for the traditional independence assumption on the quantized data. It is proven that the remote estimate achieves asymptotic normality, and the error covariance matrix converges to the original CRLB, confirming its asymptotic efficiency. Compared to existing identification algorithms under one-bit data, this method reduces the asymptotic mean squared error by at least 36%. Several numerical examples are simulated to show the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [172] [Gradient-robustness in optimization subject to stationary Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12028)
*Constanze Neutsch,Winnifried Wollner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文讨论了非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题模拟及其最优控制的梯度鲁棒离散化方法，比较了不同连续等价公式的梯度鲁棒离散化，并分析了公式选择对最优控制问题中梯度计算所需伴随方程的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于开发适用于非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题模拟及其最优控制的梯度鲁棒离散化方法。由于不同连续等价公式在离散化时可能表现出不同的数值特性，特别是对梯度计算的鲁棒性，因此需要系统比较各种公式的离散化效果，并分析其对最优控制问题中伴随方程的影响。

Method: 方法包括：1）考虑非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题的多种连续等价公式；2）对这些公式进行梯度鲁棒离散化；3）比较不同公式离散化后的数值特性；4）分析公式选择对最优控制问题中梯度计算所需伴随方程的影响。

Result: 研究结果应包括：1）不同连续等价公式的梯度鲁棒离散化特性比较；2）各种离散化方法在数值模拟中的表现；3）公式选择对伴随方程形式和计算复杂度的影响；4）最优控制问题中梯度计算的准确性和效率评估。

Conclusion: 结论是：非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes问题的不同连续等价公式在梯度鲁棒离散化方面存在显著差异，公式选择直接影响最优控制问题中伴随方程的形式和梯度计算的数值特性，为实际应用中的公式选择和离散化策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: In this article, we discuss gradient robust discretizations for the simulation of non-linear incompressible Navier-Stokes problem and the optimal control of such flow. We consider several formulations of the flow problem that are equivalent for the continuous non-linear forward problem and compare their gradient robust discretization. We will then discuss the influence of the chosen formulation on the adjoint equations needed for gradient computation in the solution of the optimal control problem.

</details>


### [173] [Well-posedness of boundary control systems and application to ISS for coupled heat equations with boundary disturbances and delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12029)
*Yassine El Gantouh,Jun Zheng,Guchuan Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一类边界控制系统的解的存在性和适定性，提出了保证解对初始数据和L^p输入连续依赖的显式可验证条件，并应用于边界耦合热方程的指数输入到状态稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究边界控制系统的适定性问题，特别是解对初始数据和输入的连续依赖性，这对于无限维系统的控制理论具有重要意义。现有方法在处理无界控制算子时存在局限性，需要开发新的分析技术。

Method: 提出了线性时不变无限维系统输入/输出映射的新有界性估计方法，该技术适用于具有无界控制和观测算子的系统。利用这一估计证明了边界控制系统解的存在性和适定性条件。

Result: 建立了保证解对初始数据和L^p输入连续依赖的显式可验证条件。将所开发的技术应用于边界耦合热方程，推导出具有边界扰动和时滞的系统的指数输入到状态稳定性的具体条件。

Conclusion: 论文为具有无界控制算子的边界控制系统提供了系统的适定性分析框架，所提出的有界性估计技术具有广泛的应用价值，特别是在处理边界耦合热方程等具体问题时表现出有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies the existence of solutions and, in particular, the well-posedness of a class of boundary control systems. Our main result provides explicit and verifiable conditions on the system data that guarantee continuous dependence of solutions on the initial data and $L^p$-inputs. The proof relies on a new boundedness estimate for the input/output maps of linear time-invariant infinite-dimensional systems with unbounded control and observation operators. The developed technique is applied to derive specific conditions for the exponential input-to-state stability of boundary-coupled heat equations with boundary disturbances and time-delays.

</details>


### [174] [Controlled Swarm Gradient Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12039)
*Louison Aubert*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将受控模拟退火框架扩展到群体梯度动力学，通过构建速度场使边际分布精确跟踪退火曲线，实现任意速率的全局优化收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展受控模拟退火框架到群体梯度动力学，解决非凸势函数的全局优化问题，利用噪声强度依赖于过程边际密度的Langevin型平均场扩散

Method: 基于Huang和Malik的时间齐次模型分析不变概率密度，证明其收敛到全局最小化支撑集；构建速度场解连续性方程，叠加到群体梯度动力学得到受控过程

Result: 不变概率密度在逆温度参数趋于无穷时弱收敛到全局最小化支撑集；受控群体动力学可精确跟踪任意退火曲线，理论上实现任意速率的全局收敛

Conclusion: 受控群体梯度动力学框架为全局优化提供新方法，允许任意快速收敛，但数值实现存在限制，性能与受控模拟退火相比有差异

Abstract: We consider the global optimization of a non-convex potential $U : \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}$ and extend the controlled simulated annealing framework introduced by Molin et al. (2026) to the class of swarm gradient dynamics, a family of Langevin-type mean-field diffusions whose noise intensity depends locally on the marginal density of the process. Building on the time-homogeneous model of Huang and Malik (2025), we first analyze its invariant probability density and show that, as the inverse temperature parameter tends to infinity, it converges weakly to a probability measure supported on the set of global minimizers of $U$. This result justifies using this family of invariant measures as an annealing curve in a controlled swarm setting. Given an arbitrary non-decreasing cooling schedule, we then prove the existence of a velocity field solving the continuity equation associated with the curve of invariant densities. Superimposing this field onto the swarm gradient dynamics yields a well-posed controlled process whose marginal law follows exactly the prescribed annealing curve. As a consequence, the controlled swarm dynamics converges toward global minimizers with, in principle, arbitrarily fast convergence rates, entirely dictated by the choice of the cooling schedule. Finally, we discuss an algorithmic implementation of the controlled dynamics and compare its performance with controlled simulated annealing, highlighting some numerical limitations.

</details>


### [175] [Expanding Flow Shop Tasks Based on Recursive Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12077)
*Boris Kupriyanov,Alexander Lazarev,Alexandr Roschin,Frank Werner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文探讨了流水车间调度问题的递归表示扩展，展示了递归函数如何统一描述多种扩展问题，提出了三类递归函数及其叠加结构，并建立了递归函数的一般要求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流水车间调度问题存在多种扩展形式，传统方法需要为每种扩展单独建模。作者旨在开发一种统一的递归表示框架，能够简洁地描述多种扩展问题，提高问题建模的通用性和灵活性。

Method: 提出了三类递归函数：与机器相关的函数、基于约束调整处理时间的函数、控制调度可行性的函数。分析了这些函数的叠加结构，展示了如何用递归函数描述目标函数，建立了递归函数的一般要求和性质，并通过简单扩展和分支定界优化示例演示了新问题的表述。

Result: 成功开发了一个统一的递归表示框架，能够描述六种扩展问题。展示了递归函数如何通过叠加组合形成复杂调度问题的表示，建立了递归函数的一般规范，并通过实例验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 递归函数为流水车间调度问题的扩展提供了统一、灵活的建模框架。该框架能够简洁地描述多种扩展问题，为调度问题的形式化表示和优化算法设计提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: The paper discusses several extensions of the recursive representation of the flow shop scheduling problem. It is shown that recursive functions make it possible to describe multiple extensions in a single problem. The paper considers altogether six extensions. The examples consider three types of recursive functions: functions associated with the machine, functions that adjust the procession time based on constraints, and functions that control the feasibility of the schedule. The structure of the superpositions of these functions is presented, and also descriptions of several objective functions by recursive functions are presented. Then the general requirements for a recursive function are formulated and its properties are described. Finally, a demonstration of the formulation of new problems is provided using examples of simple flow shop extensions and branch and bound optimization.

</details>


### [176] [Asymptotic Convergence of the Frank-Wolfe Algorithm for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12104)
*Matthew Hough*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文分析了Frank-Wolfe算法在单调算子下求解变分不等式的收敛性，通过连续时间插值和动力系统理论证明了算法的渐近收敛性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Frank-Wolfe算法在单调算子下求解变分不等式的收敛性质，特别是针对Hammond关于广义虚构博弈收敛性的猜想。

Method: 采用连续时间插值方法将离散迭代转化为连续动力系统，利用动力系统理论分析渐近行为，从而推导原始离散算法的收敛结果。

Result: 证明了Frank-Wolfe算法的收敛性：迭代点的每个聚点都是变分不等式的解；迭代点到解集的距离收敛到零；Frank-Wolfe间隙渐近消失。在强单调情形下，解唯一且迭代收敛到该解。

Conclusion: 该研究为Frank-Wolfe算法在单调算子下的收敛性提供了理论保证，并特别证明了Hammond关于广义虚构博弈收敛性的猜想。

Abstract: We consider the Frank-Wolfe algorithm for solving variational inequalities over compact, convex sets under a monotone $C^1$ operator and vanishing, nonsummable step sizes. We introduce a continuous-time interpolation of the discrete iteration and use tools from dynamical systems theory to analyze its asymptotic behavior. This allows us to derive convergence results for the original discrete algorithm. Consequently, every cluster point of the iterates is a solution of the underlying variational inequality, the distance from the iterates to the solution set converges to zero, and the Frank-Wolfe gap vanishes asymptotically. In the strongly monotone case, the solution is unique and the iterates converge to it. In particular, this proves Hammond's conjecture on the convergence of generalized fictitious play.

</details>


### [177] [Folding Mixed-Integer Linear Programs and Reflection Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12136)
*Rolf van der Hulst*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文扩展了DRCR方法，使其能处理反射对称性并应用于混合整数线性规划，通过仿射全幺模分解减少整数变量，实验证明能有效提升求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性对分支定界和线性优化算法性能有负面影响，现有DRCR方法仅能处理置换对称性，需要扩展到更一般的反射对称性并应用于混合整数线性规划。

Method: 1. 扩展DRCR处理反射对称性；2. 将DRCR应用于混合整数线性规划的连续变量；3. 使用仿射全幺模分解重新表述混合整数线性规划以减少整数变量数量。

Result: 在MIPLIB 2017数据集上使用SCIP 10的实验表明：对于线性规划松弛，带反射对称性的DRCR相比原始DRCR能适度减少运行时间；对于混合整数线性规划模型，DRCR相比SCIP默认配置能显著减少求解时间；检测算法快速且能扩展到大规模问题实例。

Conclusion: DRCR是处理对称性的有效工具，扩展后的方法能处理更广泛的对称类型并应用于混合整数线性规划，显著提升求解效率，算法具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: For mixed-integer linear programming and linear programming it is well known that symmetries can have a negative impact on the performance of branch-and-bound and linear optimization algorithms. A common strategy to handle symmetries in linear programs is to reduce the dimension of the linear program by aggregating symmetric variables and solving a linear program of reduced dimension. In their work ``Dimension Reduction via Color Refinement'' (DRCR), Grohe, Kersting, Mladenov and Selman show that it is sufficient to run a fast color refinement algorithm to detect permutation symmetries and reduce the dimension of the linear program. We extend DRCR in two directions. First, we show that DRCR can be extended to reflection symmetries, which generalize permutation symmetries. Second, we show the folklore result that DRCR can be applied to the continuous columns of mixed-integer linear programs. In order to derive additional reductions on the integer variables we use affine totally unimodular decompositions to reformulate mixed-integer linear programs into mixed-integer linear programs with fewer integer variables. Computational experiments on MIPLIB 2017 collection set using SCIP 10 show that DRCR is an effective tool for handling symmetries. For the linear programming relaxations, DRCR with reflection symmetries yields a modest reduction in running time compared to the original DRCR procedure. For mixed-integer linear programming models, DRCR is very effective at reducing the solution time compared to the default configuration of SCIP. Moreover, the developed DRCR detection algorithms are fast and scale well to large problem instances.

</details>


### [178] [Operator Splitting, Policy Iteration, and Machine Learning for Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12167)
*Alain Bensoussan,Thien P. B. Nguyen,Minh-Binh Tran,Son N. T. Tu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种分裂方法求解二阶Hamilton-Jacobi方程，将其分解为热传导步骤和纯一阶步骤，后者采用梯度值策略迭代算法实现，利用基于特征的机器学习方法，建立了收敛率分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决二阶Hamilton-Jacobi方程的数值计算问题，传统方法面临高维空间中的计算复杂性和收敛性挑战。通过分裂方法将二阶问题分解为更易处理的子问题，结合机器学习技术提高计算效率。

Method: 采用分裂方法将二阶Hamilton-Jacobi方程分解为热传导步骤和纯一阶步骤。一阶步骤使用梯度值策略迭代算法实现，通过求解线性特征方程和学习值函数来最小化加权值梯度损失。该方法结合了基于特征的机器学习技术。

Result: 建立了分裂方法的收敛率：对于Lipschitz初始数据，L∞误差下界为O(h)，上界为O(h^{1/7})；对于半凹数据改进为O(h^{1/5})；对于C²数据改进为O(h^{1/3})。在周期设置中，L¹误差上界为O(h^{1/2})。一阶步骤的加权L²误差分析显示指数收敛。

Conclusion: 提出的分裂方法结合梯度值策略迭代算法能够有效求解二阶Hamilton-Jacobi方程，提供了理论收敛保证，数值结果稳定准确，为高维问题提供了可行的计算框架。

Abstract: We propose a splitting approach to solve the second-order Hamilton--Jacobi equation, reducing it to a heat step and a purely first-order step. The latter is implemented using a gradient value policy iteration algorithm, enabling efficient characteristic-based machine learning methods. We establish convergence rates for the splitting method. In particular, the $L^\infty$ error is bounded below by $\mathcal{O}(h)$ and above by $\mathcal{O}(h^{1/7})$ for Lipschitz initial data; this improves to $\mathcal{O}(h^{1/5})$ for semiconcave data and to $\mathcal{O}(h^{1/3})$ for $C^2$ data. We also prove an upper $L^1$ error estimate of order $\mathcal{O}(h^{1/2})$ in the periodic setting, where $h$ is the splitting step. For the first-order step, we provide a weighted $L^2$ error analysis that shows exponential convergence. Each iteration solves linear characteristic equations and learns the value function by minimizing a weighted value gradient loss. The approach yields stable and accurate numerical results.

</details>
